- **ata-lowest-tolerable-hc**: Recommends using the lowest tolerable hydrocortisone dose to potentially decrease risks of metabolic and cardiovascular disease in central adrenal insufficiency. Triggers include managing a patient with central adrenal insufficiency requiring glucocorticoid replacement. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/adrenal/ata-lowest-tolerable-hc) · ★14)
- **ata-lt4-dosing-ch**: Determines levothyroxine dosing for central hypothyroidism to achieve serum free T4 in the mid to upper half of the reference range. Triggers include confirmed central hypothyroidism requiring thyroid hormone replacement when initiating L‑4 therapy. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pituitary/ata-lt4-dosing-ch) · ★14)
- **ata-lt4-overreplacement-fracture**: Monitors levothyroxine (L-T4) replacement in hypopituitarism to prevent over-replacement and associated fracture risk. Triggered when managing L-T4 therapy, especially when levothyroxine dose adjustment is needed to avoid overdose. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/metabolic-bone/ata-lt4-overreplacement-fracture) · ★14)
- **ata-lt4-target-range-ch**: Guides levothyroxine (L-T4) dosing and monitoring in adults with central hypothyroidism (CH) from pituitary or hypothalamic disease, targeting serum free T4 in the mid to upper half of the reference range at about 1.6 μg/kg/d and adjusting with fT4, not TSH. Trigger phrases: "central hypothyroidism", "secondary hypothyroidism levothyroxine dose", "hypopituitarism fT4 target", "TSH undetectable on levothyroxine", "starting GH or estrogen in CH". ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pituitary/ata-lt4-target-range-ch) · ★14)
- **ata-major-stress-hc-regimen**: Administers 100-mg hydrocortisone IV bolus followed by continuous IV infusion of 200 mg HC per 24 hours (alternatively 50 mg IV every 6 hours) for patients undergoing major surgical stress. Use when managing patient with major surgical stress; triggers include patient undergoing major surgery. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/adrenal/ata-major-stress-hc-regimen) · ★14)
- **ata-male-hypogonadism-labs**: This skill guides ordering initial labs (serum testosterone, FSH, and LH) to evaluate suspected central hypogonadism in adult males. Use when a male presents with low libido, fatigue, decreased energy, or other signs suggestive of gonadal deficiency. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/ata-male-hypogonadism-labs) · ★14)
- **ata-male-hypogonadism-timing**: Recommends performing hormonal testing for central hypogonadism in males in the absence of acute/subacute illness, before 10 AM after an overnight fast, combined with serum prolactin measurement. Use when scheduling hormonal tests for male hypogonadism; triggers include preparing to order hypogonadism labs in male patients. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/ata-male-hypogonadism-timing) · ★14)
- **ata-male-testosterone-replacement-indications**: Recommends testosterone replacement for adult males with central hypogonadism and no contraindications to prevent anemia related to T deficiency; reduce fat mass; and improve BMD, libido, sexual function, energy levels, sense of well-being, and muscle mass and strength. Triggers include consideration of testosterone replacement in males with central hypogonadism (e.g., low serum testosterone with symptoms of deficiency). ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/ata-male-testosterone-replacement-indications) · ★14)
- **ata-mild-ch-management**: Manages suspected mild central hypothyroidism in patients with pituitary disease and low-normal free thyroxine (fT4). Initiates levothyroxine (L-T4) when suggestive symptoms are present or when serial fT4 shows a decrease of 20% or more. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pituitary/ata-mild-ch-management) · ★14)
- **ata-minor-moderate-stress-hc**: Suggests administering 25-75 mg hydrocortisone per 24 hours for 1-2 days in patients with adrenal insufficiency undergoing minor to moderate surgical stress. Triggers include patient undergoing minor to moderate surgery with known or suspected adrenal insufficiency. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/adrenal/ata-minor-moderate-stress-hc) · ★14)
- **ata-no-prescheduled-ddavp-postop**: Recommends against prescheduled desmopressin (DDAVP) dosing in the first week after pituitary surgery due to hyponatremia risk from transient diabetes insipidus resolution and syndrome of inappropriate antidiuretic hormone secretion occurring 7–10 days postoperatively. Use when managing a postoperative diabetes insipidus patient in the first week after surgery. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pituitary/ata-no-prescheduled-ddavp-postop) · ★14)
- **ata-non-catecholamine-ppgl-diagnostic-workup**: Orders mandatory MRI, contrast CT, and somatostatin receptor nuclear imaging when a non-catecholamine-producing pheochromocytoma or paraganglioma is suspected based on imaging findings with normal fractionated metanephrines and catecholamines. Considers SDHD germline genetic testing in hereditary head/neck paraganglioma cases triggered by multifocal or familial head/neck lesions. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/phaeochromocytoma/ata-non-catecholamine-ppgl-diagnostic-workup) · ★14)
- **ata-notarial-cpc-384**: Usar para pesquisar ou analisar: Ata Notarial (CPC 384). Combinar com o protocolo LegalBR e conferir fontes oficiais antes de aplicar. ([prof-ramos/skills](https://github.com/prof-ramos/skills/tree/HEAD/legalbr/materia/processo-civil/ata-notarial-cpc-384))
- **ata-notarial-de-constatacao**: Conduz ata notarial de constatação com base no CPC art. 384 e na exclusividade da Lei 8.935/1994, delimitando fatos percebidos pessoalmente, competência, diligência, cadeia de evidência e três cláusulas propostas sem criar eficácia jurídica. Esta skill deve ser usada quando o pedido mencionar ata notarial, constatação de fato, prova digital, conteúdo de site ou mensagem, diligência, e-Not Provas, alerta de falsidade, cláusula de não-eficácia ou descrição imparcial pelo tabelião. ([sbroggioadv/tabelionato-os-marketplace](https://github.com/sbroggioadv/tabelionato-os-marketplace/tree/HEAD/tabelionato-os/skills/ata-notarial-de-constatacao))
- **ata-oral-estrogen-gh-dose**: Suggests women on oral estrogen replacement receive higher GH doses compared to eugonadal females or males. Triggers when managing GH replacement in a woman on oral estrogen requiring GH therapy (e.g., "female on oral estrogen needing GH replacement"). ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pituitary/ata-oral-estrogen-gh-dose) · ★14)
- **ata-pediatric-ppgl-approach**: Investigates for pheochromocytoma and paraganglioma in children presenting with hypertension plus symptoms such as headache, excessive sweating, or palpitations. Notes the absence of pediatric reference values for endocrine tests and selects tumor resection as first-line treatment when triggered by pediatric hypertension with associated symptoms like headache or excessive sweating. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/phaeochromocytoma/ata-pediatric-ppgl-approach) · ★14)
- **ata-pituitary-apoplexy-gc-until-diagnosis**: Administers glucocorticoid coverage in patients with pituitary apoplexy to prevent adrenal crisis while awaiting definitive laboratory diagnosis. Triggers include managing a pituitary apoplexy patient needing glucocorticoid coverage until laboratory confirmation of pituitary function. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pituitary/ata-pituitary-apoplexy-gc-until-diagnosis) · ★14)
- **ata-pituitary-apoplexy-pituitary-insufficiency-test**: Recommends testing for acute pituitary insufficiency in all patients with pituitary apoplexy. Use when managing pituitary apoplexy patient; triggers include pituitary apoplexy diagnosis. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pituitary/ata-pituitary-apoplexy-pituitary-insufficiency-test) · ★14)
- **ata-post-acromegaly-di-monitoring**: Monitors for diabetes insipidus development after glucocorticoid replacement in cured acromegaly patients; recommends adrenal insufficiency testing if diabetes insipidus improves without an adrenal insufficiency diagnosis. Triggered when managing a cured acromegaly patient receiving glucocorticoid replacement. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pituitary/ata-post-acromegaly-di-monitoring) · ★14)
- **ata-post-acth-tumor-gc**: Recommend continuing glucocorticoid replacement until hypothalamic-pituitary-adrenal (HPA) axis recovery after surgical resection of ACTH-secreting pituitary tumors. Use when managing postoperative patients following ACTH-secreting tumor surgery to prevent adrenal insufficiency during HPA axis recovery. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pituitary/ata-post-acth-tumor-gc) · ★14)
- **ata-post-cushing-axis-retest**: Retest thyroid and GH axes after curative surgery for Cushing's disease before initiating hormone replacement. Use when managing postoperative Cushing's disease patient; triggers include postoperative Cushing's surgery patient needing hormone reassessment. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pituitary/ata-post-cushing-axis-retest) · ★14)
- **ata-postmenopausal-gonadotrope-diagnosis**: Diagnoses gonadotrope dysfunction in postmenopausal women by confirming absent high serum FSH and LH when not on hormone replacement therapy. Use when evaluating a postmenopausal woman for hypogonadism; triggers include postmenopausal woman with suspected hypogonadism. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pituitary/ata-postmenopausal-gonadotrope-diagnosis) · ★14)
- **ata-postop-gc-individualized**: In patients with normal preoperative adrenal function, suggests an individualized clinical approach for postoperative glucocorticoid administration until HPA axis evaluation can be performed. Triggers include managing postoperative patient with normal preoperative adrenal function needing glucocorticoid management. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/adrenal/ata-postop-gc-individualized) · ★14)
- **ata-postop-pituitary-apoplexy-monitoring**: Recommends that clinicians monitor pituitary axes in pituitary apoplexy patients treated with surgical decompression or conservative management because hypopituitarism may develop over time. Use when managing pituitary apoplexy patient; triggers include pituitary apoplexy patient needing longitudinal monitoring. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pituitary/ata-postop-pituitary-apoplexy-monitoring) · ★14)
- **ata-postop-pituitary-axis-surveillance**: Recommends retesting all pituitary axes starting at 6 weeks after pituitary surgery and periodically to monitor development or resolution of pituitary deficiencies. Use when managing postoperative pituitary surgery patient; triggers include postoperative pituitary surgery patient needing longitudinal hormone monitoring. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pituitary/ata-postop-pituitary-axis-surveillance) · ★14)
- **ata-postop-thyroid-function-check**: Measures free T4 6–8 weeks after pituitary surgery in patients with intact preoperative thyroid function to detect central hypothyroidism. Triggers include postoperative patient needing thyroid function assessment and postoperative patient with intact preoperative thyroid function. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pituitary/ata-postop-thyroid-function-check) · ★14)
- **ata-ppgl-avoid-fna-hnpgl**: Recommends against fine needle cytology for pathological diagnosis of head and neck paraganglioma due to its low accuracy and risk of misdiagnosis. Use when a clinician asks 'Should I perform fine needle cytology for suspected head and neck paraganglioma?' — triggers include suspicion of head and neck paraganglioma requiring pathological confirmation. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/phaeochromocytoma/ata-ppgl-avoid-fna-hnpgl) · ★14)
- **ata-ppgl-biomarker-use-for-prognosis**: This skill guides the use of biomarkers to prognosticate metastatic pheochromocytoma and paraganglioma (PPGL) by applying independent prognostic factors such as older age, larger tumor size, metastases at diagnosis, multiple metastases, high chromogranin A, plasma fractionated metanephrines ≥5× ULN, or elevated plasma 3‑MT; for head‑and‑neck PPGL, only high blood 3‑MT predicts disease‑specific survival. It is triggered when a clinician asks about prognosis based on biomarker results or encounters elevated biomarker levels in a PPGL patient. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/phaeochromocytoma/ata-ppgl-biomarker-use-for-prognosis) · ★14)
- **ata-ppgl-bone-metastasis-management**: This skill guides management of bone metastases in pheochromocytoma and paraganglioma, recommending external radiation therapy and surgical approaches similar to other solid cancers. It is triggered by known or suspected bone metastases, spinal cord compression symptoms, bone pain, pathological fracture, or hypercalcemia in a PPGL patient, emphasizing prompt diagnosis/treatment for spinal cord compression and consideration of bone-modifying agents for skeletal-related event prevention. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/phaeochromocytoma/ata-ppgl-bone-metastasis-management) · ★14)
- **ata-ppgl-chemotherapy-indication**: Recommends CVD chemotherapy as first-line systemic treatment for unresectable pheochromocytoma/paraganglioma with rapid progression or when radionuclide therapy is not applicable. Considers disease progression rate and feasibility to choose between CVD chemotherapy and radionuclide therapy in patients with positive nuclear imaging when progression is slow and radionuclide therapy feasible. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/phaeochromocytoma/ata-ppgl-chemotherapy-indication) · ★14)
- **ata-ppgl-confirmatory-test-selection**: Selects the appropriate confirmatory test after a positive pheochromocytoma/paraganglioma (PPGL) screening test. Triggers include elevated random urinary metanephrines (&gt;3× ULN after creatinine correction), blood catecholamines (&gt;3× ULN), or plasma-free fractionated metanephrines (&gt;ULN). ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/phaeochromocytoma/ata-ppgl-confirmatory-test-selection) · ★14)
- **ata-ppgl-external-beam-radiation-indication**: Recommends stereotactic radiotherapy for local control of head and neck paraganglioma and postoperative radiotherapy for incompletely resectable spinal paraganglioma; considers external beam radiation therapy for local control of pheochromocytoma and paraganglioma outside the head and neck. Indicated when a clinician asks whether to recommend radiation therapy for local control in a PPGL patient, triggered by unresectable or incompletely resected PPGL lesion. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/phaeochromocytoma/ata-ppgl-external-beam-radiation-indication) · ★14)
- **ata-ppgl-genetic-marker-interpretation-for-mets**: This skill interprets SDHx and fumarate hydratase gene variants to assess metastasis risk in pheochromocytoma and paraganglioma patients. Triggered by identification of SDHB pathogenic variant (or negative SDHB immunostaining), FH variant, or other SDHx mutations when a clinician asks how genetic results affect metastasis risk assessment. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/phaeochromocytoma/ata-ppgl-genetic-marker-interpretation-for-mets) · ★14)
- **ata-ppgl-genetic-testing-indication**: Indicates and performs genetic testing for pheochromocytoma and paraganglioma when a clinician questions whether to order testing for a PPGL patient, using triggers such as any PPGL diagnosis, family history of PPGL, young age at onset (&lt;50 years), bilateral adrenal tumors, or extra-adrenal (paraganglioma) location. It guides collection of detailed family and medical history, considers SDHB testing for high metastatic risk (35–75%), informs patients about hereditary PPGL significance, and recommends testing after counseling at a quality‑guaranteed facility, noting that testing is not covered… ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/phaeochromocytoma/ata-ppgl-genetic-testing-indication) · ★14)
- **ata-ppgl-genetic-variant-treatment-algorithm**: This skill selects a treatment algorithm for pheochromocytoma and paraganglioma based on identified germline pathogenic variants, enabling personalized management per syndrome-specific guidelines. It is triggered when a clinician identifies RET, VHL, NF1, or SDHx pathogenic variants and asks how genetic results should change management. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/phaeochromocytoma/ata-ppgl-genetic-variant-treatment-algorithm) · ★14)
- **ata-ppgl-gi-symptom-management**: Manages gastrointestinal symptoms from catecholamine excess in pheochromocytoma and paraganglioma, addressing constipation, paralytic ileus, megacolon, or intestinal pseudo-obstruction/perforation. Triggered by clinician queries about treating GI symptoms in PPGL patients with constipation, abdominal distension, or nausea/vomiting. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/phaeochromocytoma/ata-ppgl-gi-symptom-management) · ★14)
- **ata-ppgl-histopathological-confirmation**: This skill confirms pheochromocytoma and paraganglioma (PPGL) diagnosis on histopathology by requiring immunostaining positive for Chromogranin A and negative for cytokeratin in tumor tissue, with SDHB negativity indicating SDHx pathogenic variants that predict metastasis or recurrence. It is triggered when a clinician asks 'How do I confirm pheochromocytoma diagnosis on pathology?' or encounters resected adrenal or paraganglioma tissue needing a definitive diagnosis. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/phaeochromocytoma/ata-ppgl-histopathological-confirmation) · ★14)
- **ata-ppgl-histopathological-scoring-for-mets**: Applies histopathological scoring systems (GAPP, COPPS, PASS) to estimate metastasis risk in pheochromocytoma and paraganglioma. Used when a clinician asks 'What is the metastasis risk based on this pheochromocytoma pathology report?' and triggered by availability of histopathological specimen from PPGL resection. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/phaeochromocytoma/ata-ppgl-histopathological-scoring-for-mets) · ★14)
- **ata-ppgl-hypertensive-crisis-management**: Administers intravenous phentolamine followed by infusion, then transitions to oral doxazosin after the acute phase resolves, while avoiding β-blockers before α-blockade. Indicated for severe hypertension (&gt;180/110 mmHg) with headache, palpitations, or diaphoresis in known or suspected pheochromocytoma or paraganglioma. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/phaeochromocytoma/ata-ppgl-hypertensive-crisis-management) · ★14)
- **ata-ppgl-imaging-modality-selection**: Selects the initial imaging modality (CT, MRI, 123I-MIBG scintigraphy, or 18F-FDG PET) for suspected pheochromocytoma and paraganglioma based on clinical question (tumor localization vs metastasis detection) and patient factors. Triggered by biochemical confirmation of PPGL or strong clinical suspicion when clinicians ask 'What imaging test should I order for suspected pheochromocytoma?' ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/phaeochromocytoma/ata-ppgl-imaging-modality-selection) · ★14)
- **ata-ppgl-medical-treatment-initiation**: Initiates and adjusts medical treatment for functional pheochromocytoma and paraganglioma. Starts with selective α-blocker for catecholamine excess hypertension or symptoms, adds calcium antagonists or metyrosine if BP control insufficient, and adds β-blockers only after adequate α-blockade for tachycardia/tachyarrhythmia, myocardial damage, heart failure, or ischemic heart disease. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/phaeochromocytoma/ata-ppgl-medical-treatment-initiation) · ★14)
- **ata-ppgl-metastasis-risk-assessment**: Assesses metastasis risk in pheochromocytoma and paraganglioma using age at diagnosis, tumor size, catecholamine secretion pattern, timing of metastatic lesion diagnosis, tumor localization, disease type, histopathological findings, SDHB gene pathogenic variants, and SDHB immunohistochemistry status. Use when a clinician asks 'What is the metastasis risk for this pheochromocytoma patient?' Triggers include new PPGL diagnosis or change in clinical status during follow-up. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/phaeochromocytoma/ata-ppgl-metastasis-risk-assessment) · ★14)
- **ata-ppgl-pain-management-precautions**: Coordinates multidisciplinary treatment with palliative care unit for pain management while avoiding medications that risk inducing hypertensive crisis (e.g., metoclopramide, certain antidepressants) in PPGL patients. Triggered by reports of pain in PPGL patient, especially with bone involvement. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/phaeochromocytoma/ata-ppgl-pain-management-precautions) · ★14)
- **ata-ppgl-perioperative-volume-management**: Manages perioperative volume for pheochromocytoma and paraganglioma by initiating a normal salt diet (9 g/day) on day 3 of α‑blocker therapy, titrating intake to blood pressure and orthostatic changes, and administering 1–2 L saline pre‑operatively. Triggered when planning fluid/salt management for scheduled PPGL resection. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/phaeochromocytoma/ata-ppgl-perioperative-volume-management) · ★14)
- **ata-ppgl-preoperative-assessment-unclear-overproduction**: Determines that α-blockers are generally unnecessary when catecholamine overproduction is unclear in suspected pheochromocytoma or paraganglioma, but requires comprehensive assessment of clinical findings before prescribing α-blockers before surgery. Use when a clinician asks 'Should I prescribe α-blockers preoperatively for this patient with suspected but unconfirmed pheochromocytoma?' Triggers include uncertain biochemical evidence of PPGL despite clinical suspicion. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/phaeochromocytoma/ata-ppgl-preoperative-assessment-unclear-overproduction) · ★14)
- **ata-ppgl-radionuclide-therapy-selection**: The skill selects 131I-MIBG therapy for pheochromocytoma or paraganglioma with positive 123I-MIBG scintigraphy that reduces excess catecholamines, or 177Lu-DOTATATE therapy for lesions with positive somatostatin receptor scintigraphy, both modalities covered by Japanese health insurance. It is invoked when a clinician asks which radionuclide therapy to use for metastatic or unresectable PPGL amenable to targeted treatment. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/phaeochromocytoma/ata-ppgl-radionuclide-therapy-selection) · ★14)
- **ata-ppgl-screening-test-selection**: Chooses the initial screening test for suspected pheochromocytoma and paraganglioma when evaluating hypertension with paroxysmal symptoms, adrenal incidentaloma, or family history of PPGL. Selects among random urinary fractionated metanephrines (creatinine corrected), blood fractionated catecholamines, or plasma-free fractionated metanephrines. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/phaeochromocytoma/ata-ppgl-screening-test-selection) · ★14)
- **ata-ppgl-surgical-approach-selection**: Selects surgical approach for pheochromocytoma and paraganglioma resection based on tumor size, invasion suspicion, metastatic status, and familial bilateral risk when a clinician asks 'What surgical approach should I use for this pheochromocytoma patient?' Triggers include confirmed PPGL requiring surgical intervention. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/phaeochromocytoma/ata-ppgl-surgical-approach-selection) · ★14)
- **ata-ppgl-surveillance-intensity-determination**: This skill determines postoperative surveillance intensity for pheochromocytoma and paraganglioma based on hereditary status, catecholamine phenotype, tumor size, and location. Use when a clinician asks 'How long and how often should I follow up this pheochromocytoma patient after surgery?' with triggers such as completed PPGL resection, hereditary PPGL, sporadic PPGL with noradrenaline dominance, tumor diameter ≥5 cm, or extra-adrenal location outside head/neck. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/phaeochromocytoma/ata-ppgl-surveillance-intensity-determination) · ★14)
- **ata-ppgl-thyroid-block-for-mibg**: The skill recommends administering potassium iodide (50–150 mg/day) or Lugol's solution (5–10 drops/day) for three days prior to and continuing through the day of 123I-MIBG scintigraphy to reduce thyroid radiation exposure and prevent medullary thyroid cancer in patients undergoing evaluation for pheochromocytoma or paraganglioma. It is triggered when a clinician questions whether to give a thyroid blocking agent for a 123I-MIBG scan, such as when ordering 123I-MIBG scintigraphy for PPGL evaluation. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/phaeochromocytoma/ata-ppgl-thyroid-block-for-mibg) · ★14)
- **ata-pregnant-ppgl-management**: Manage pheochromocytoma and paraganglioma in pregnancy by preferring ultrasound or MRI for imaging (avoiding CT/123I-MIBG unless benefits outweigh radiation risks), using selective α-blockers as first-line medical therapy (adding calcium antagonists if needed before delivery), and recommending tumor resection in the second trimester up to 24 weeks gestation when feasible. Triggers include pregnancy with hypertension, headaches, palpitations, or adrenal mass, and the clinician question “How should I manage a pregnant patient with suspected or confirmed pheochromocytoma?” ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/phaeochromocytoma/ata-pregnant-ppgl-management) · ★14)
- **ata-premenopausal-women-gonadal-hormone-treatment**: Recommends gonadal hormone treatment for premenopausal women with central hypogonadism when no contraindications exist. Triggered when evaluating hormonal replacement in a premenopausal woman with oligomenorrhea or amenorrhea suggestive of central hypogonadism. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/hypogonadism/ata-premenopausal-women-gonadal-hormone-treatment) · ★14)
- **ata-preop-ch-lt4**: This skill guides perioperative levothyroxine management for adults with preoperative central hypothyroidism undergoing non-emergency surgery. Trigger phrases include "preoperative central hypothyroidism patient requiring non-emergency surgery" and "use when managing preoperative CH patient". ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pituitary/ata-preop-ch-lt4) · ★14)
- **ata-preop-stress-dosing**: Recommends administering stress-dose glucocorticoids before surgery and tapering after surgery in patients with adrenal insufficiency before repeat testing. Triggers include preoperative planning for surgery in a patient with known or suspected adrenal insufficiency. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/adrenal/ata-preop-stress-dosing) · ★14)
- **ata-prolactinoma-axis-reassess**: Recommends reassessing all pituitary axes in patients with macroprolactinoma and central hypogonadism who have had successful dopamine agonist treatment. Use when managing a macroprolactinoma patient after dopamine agonist treatment with normalized prolactin and tumor shrinkage. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pituitary/ata-prolactinoma-axis-reassess) · ★14)
- **ata-rai-decision**: Decides whether radioactive iodine (RAI) remnant ablation or adjuvant therapy is indicated after thyroidectomy for differentiated thyroid cancer, using ATA risk tier and specific pathologic features. Use when a clinician asks "does this DTC patient need RAI", "should I give radioiodine after thyroidectomy", "is RAI indicated for low risk thyroid cancer", "RAI for intermediate risk DTC", "radioactive iodine dosing for thyroid cancer", "RAI for lymph node metastases", or any question about post-surgical RAI decision-making in PTC or FTC. Source: 2015 ATA Guidelines, Haugen et al., Thyroid 2016;… ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/thyroid-cancer/ata-rai-decision) · ★14)
- **ata-random-cortisol-ai-against**: Recommends against using a random cortisol level to diagnose adrenal insufficiency. Triggers when considering a random cortisol test for AI evaluation (e.g., "random cortisol measurement being considered for AI diagnosis"). ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/adrenal/ata-random-cortisol-ai-against) · ★14)
- **ata-reuniao**: Use quando o usuário colar notas soltas de uma reunião e pedir pra organizar, resumir ou montar a ata. Sinais: anotações bagunçadas, tópicos jogados, bullets sem ordem, transcrição de call, 'resume essa reunião', 'monta a ata', 'organiza essas notas'. ([thejplabs-ai/jp-labs-skills](https://github.com/thejplabs-ai/jp-labs-skills/tree/HEAD/ata-reuniao) · ★2)
- **ata-reuniao-conselho**: Usar para redigir ou revisar: Ata de Reunião de Conselho (CA, fiscal, consultivo). Combinar com o protocolo LegalBR e conferir fontes oficiais antes de aplicar. ([prof-ramos/skills](https://github.com/prof-ramos/skills/tree/HEAD/legalbr/forma/ata-reuniao-conselho))
- **ata-stress-dose-education**: Teaches AI patients about stress-dosing and emergency glucocorticoid administration; instructs to obtain emergency card/bracelet/necklace and kit with injectable high-dose GC. Triggers when counseling any patient with adrenal insufficiency (e.g., "Use when counseling patient with adrenal insufficiency..."). ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/adrenal/ata-stress-dose-education) · ★14)
- **ata-surgery-day-gc-adjustment**: Recommends adjusting glucocorticoid doses on the day of surgery based on illness severity and magnitude of surgical stress. Triggers when managing a patient requiring surgery needing perioperative glucocorticoid management, such as "patient on HC for central AI undergoing major abdominal surgery" or "patient with hypopituitarism scheduled for minor procedure". ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/adrenal/ata-surgery-day-gc-adjustment) · ★14)
- **ata-thyroid-fna-decision**: Decides whether a thyroid nodule needs FNA biopsy using the ATA 2015 sonographic pattern classification (high/intermediate/low/very low/benign) and nodule size thresholds. Use when a clinician asks "does this thyroid nodule need a biopsy", "should I FNA this nodule", "what size threshold for thyroid FNA", "is this nodule suspicious enough for biopsy", or describes a thyroid ultrasound finding and wants a biopsy recommendation. Distinct from EU-TIRADS — uses the ATA 5-pattern system. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/thyroid-nodule/ata-thyroid-fna-decision) · ★14)
- **ata-tsh-adjustment-ch-against**: Recommends against using serum TSH levels to guide levothyroxine dose adjustments in patients with central hypothyroidism. Triggers include evaluating a central hypothyroidism patient on levothyroxine who requires dose adjustment. ([dromlakhani/MD2SKILL](https://github.com/dromlakhani/MD2SKILL/tree/HEAD/skills/endocrinology/pituitary/ata-tsh-adjustment-ch-against) · ★14)
- **ata-work**: Claim a batch, work it, release it. The middle between `/ata-work-task` (one ticket) and `/ata-list` (a whole list). ([ColaberryIntern/ColaberryEnterprise_AI_LeadershipAccelerator](https://github.com/ColaberryIntern/ColaberryEnterprise_AI_LeadershipAccelerator/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/ata-work) · ★1)
- **ata-work-task**: Claim a single ticket, get everything needed to work it, and release it when done. The trenches half of the triage board; `/ata-list` is the strategic half. ([ColaberryIntern/ColaberryEnterprise_AI_LeadershipAccelerator](https://github.com/ColaberryIntern/ColaberryEnterprise_AI_LeadershipAccelerator/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/ata-work-task) · ★1)
- **atac**: Use this skill when the user wants to send HTTP requests from the terminal — test REST APIs, manage collections and environments, or replace Postman/Insomnia with a TUI client. ([javimosch/supercli](https://github.com/javimosch/supercli/tree/HEAD/plugins/atac/skills/quickstart) · ★58)
- **atac**: Use when developing, maintaining, or running API test plans with ATAC (Arguably a Terminal API Client). Covers creating collections, writing post-request assertion scripts, running tests headlessly, managing environments, and storing test plans in a git repo. Triggers on "atac", "API test plan", "test endpoints", "run collection", "assert status code", or any programmatic HTTP endpoint testing workflow. ([shalomb/agent-skills](https://github.com/shalomb/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/atac) · ★3)
- **atac-chipseq-downstream-analysis**: Downstream analysis of ATAC-seq or ChIP-seq peak files. Annotates peaks to genomic features, computes signal summaries, finds differential peaks between conditions, and generates QC plots. ([MDhewei/bioinfor-claw](https://github.com/MDhewei/bioinfor-claw/tree/HEAD/multiomics-data-analysis/atac-chipseq-downstream-analysis) · ★65)
- **atac-differential-accessibility**: Differential accessibility testing for scATAC-seq — marker peaks via scanpy / SnapATAC2, pseudobulk DA via pyDESeq2. Use when you have clusters or conditions on an ATAC AnnData and need per-peak statistics. For RNA differential expression use scanpy.rank_genes_groups; for motif-level analysis use chromvar-motif-accessibility. ([tony-zhelonkin/scio](https://github.com/tony-zhelonkin/scio/tree/HEAD/skills/atac-differential-accessibility) · ★1)
- **atac-dnase-chipseq-peak-overlap-analysis**: Use when you have ENCODE narrowPeak files from two open-chromatin assays (e.g. ATAC-seq vs DNase-seq) in the same cell type and need QC plus overlap/benchmarking against a ChIP-seq reference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/atac-dnase-chipseq-peak-overlap-analysis))
- **atac-dnase-technology-benchmarking**: Use when contrasting open-chromatin peak files generated by different technologies (e.g. K562 ATAC-seq vs DNase-seq narrowPeak files from ENCODE) and you need ChromHMM-based annotation differences as the benchmarking readout. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/atac-dnase-technology-benchmarking))
- **atac-footprinting**: This skill performs transcription factor (TF) footprint analysis using TOBIAS on ATAC-seq data. It corrects Tn5 sequence bias, quantifies TF occupancy at motif sites, generates footprint scores, and optionally compares differential TF binding across conditions. ([diegosouzapw/awesome-omni-skill](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/awesome-omni-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/data-ai/atac-footprinting-majiayu000) · ★67 · 6 copies)
- **atac-peak-based-clustering**: Use when clustering single-cell multiome PBMC data where the ATAC modality must be reduced to its most variable peaks before PCA, and high-confidence cluster labels (PEP &lt; 0.1) are needed for downstream ARI evaluation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/atac-peak-based-clustering))
- **atac-peak-calling**: --&gt; ([mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-/tree/HEAD/Skills/Epigenomics/atac-seq/atac-peak-calling) · ★36 · 3 copies)
- **atac-peak-matrix-preprocessing**: Use when raw scATAC-seq peak-by-cell count matrices (AnnData) must be normalized and structured (peaks sorted per chromosome, intra-chromosomal pruning) before feeding scButterfly's ATAC encoder with 30% input masking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/atac-peak-matrix-preprocessing))
- **atac-qc**: --&gt; ([mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/LLMs-Universal-Life-Science-and-Clinical-Skills-/tree/HEAD/Skills/Epigenomics/atac-seq/atac-qc) · ★36 · 3 copies)
- **atac-qc-wrapper**: 1. **Assess which workflow is needed**: full pipeline or local QC summary? 2. **For full pipeline**: use `nextflow-pipeline-specialist` agent with `nf-core/atacseq`. 3. **For local QC**: ```bash python tools/atac_qc_local.py \ --fragments &lt;path&gt; \ --peaks &lt;path_or_omit&gt; \ --gtf &lt;path_or_omit&gt; \ --blacklist &lt;path_or_omit&gt; \ --output-dir &lt;output_dir&gt; ``` 4. **Document skipped metrics**: for each opt ([vsff6/bioagent-genomics](https://github.com/vsff6/bioagent-genomics/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/atac-qc-wrapper) · 2 copies)
- **atac-rna-cross-modal-correlation**: Use when single-cell multiome ATAC+RNA data has been aggregated into pseudo-bulk cell types and the goal is to test whether observed peak–gene correlations exceed a genomic-structure-aware null. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/atac-rna-cross-modal-correlation))
- **atac-seq**: ATAC-seq processing with assay QC, MACS3 peak calling, consensus peak matrices, differential accessibility, and motif or footprint follow-up. ([Runchuan-BU/BioClaw](https://github.com/Runchuan-BU/BioClaw/tree/HEAD/container/skills/atac-seq) · ★411 · 4 copies)
- **atac-seq-allele-specific-accessibility**: Detect allele-specific chromatin accessibility from ATAC-seq using WASP, GATK ASEReadCounter, or RASQUAL. Use when mapping cis-regulatory genetic variants from heterozygous SNPs, separating cis from trans regulation, building chromatin QTL (caQTL) maps, validating GWAS variant function with allelic imbalance, or detecting reference allele mapping bias before downstream analysis. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/atac-seq/allele-specific-accessibility) · ★1)
- **atac-seq-atac-peak-calling**: Call accessible chromatin regions from ATAC-seq BAM files using MACS3, MACS2, Genrich, or HMMRATAC. Use when identifying open chromatin from aligned ATAC-seq, choosing between point-source vs HMM peak callers, applying ENCODE-style pseudoreplicate IDR, removing blacklist regions, or fixing 501bp consensus peaks for downstream differential analysis. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/atac-seq/atac-peak-calling) · ★1)
- **atac-seq-atac-qc**: ATAC-seq library quality control -- TSS enrichment, FRiP, fragment-size periodicity, library complexity (NRF/PBC1/PBC2), mitochondrial fraction, and ENCODE 4 thresholds. Use when assessing whether an ATAC-seq library passes ENCODE acceptance criteria, diagnosing transposition artefacts, comparing Omni-ATAC vs standard prep quality, or selecting which replicates to drop before peak calling. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/atac-seq/atac-qc) · ★1)
- **atac-seq-bam-read-alignment-processing**: Use when when you have aligned ATAC-seq BAM files and need to quantify Tn5 transposase insertion patterns around specific genomic coordinates (motif sites, peaks, regulatory regions) to detect transcription factor occupancy footprints or compare chromatin accessibility between bound and unbound. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/atac-seq-bam-read-alignment-processing))
- **atac-seq-clustering-performance-interpretation**: Use when when you need to assess whether a given ATAC-seq clustering method (or variant) is competitive on your data or when evaluating which published method to adopt. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/atac-seq-clustering-performance-interpretation))
- **atac-seq-co-accessibility**: Infer cis-regulatory connections (peak-to-peak co-accessibility) from scATAC-seq using Cicero, ArchR getCoAccessibility, or SCENIC+. Use when linking enhancer accessibility to promoter accessibility, identifying enhancer-gene pairs from chromatin alone (without paired RNA), running gene-regulatory inference combining ATAC + RNA, or comparing predicted regulatory contacts against Hi-C/Micro-C ground truth. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/atac-seq/co-accessibility) · ★1)
- **atac-seq-consensus-peakset**: Build a differential-ready consensus peakset from per-replicate ATAC-seq peaks using iterative overlap removal, fixed-width re-centering, and majority-rule overlap. Use when generating a stable peak coordinate system for downstream differential accessibility, ML feature engineering, cross-sample comparison, or fixed-width peak counts; covers Corces 2018 iterative overlap (501 bp), DiffBind summit re-centering, and ENCODE consistency rules. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/atac-seq/consensus-peakset) · ★1)
- **atac-seq-dar-input-preparation**: Use when starting from aligned ATAC-seq data or a DiffBind contrast and you must produce a chromosome-coordinate region BED (e.g., mm10_RUNX1.bed) as the query input for BIT or comparable TR-identification methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/atac-seq-dar-input-preparation))
- **atac-seq-deep-learning-atac**: Sequence-based deep learning for ATAC-seq using chromBPNet, BPNet, scBasset, or EnFormer. Use when correcting Tn5 bias with neural networks beyond k-mer models, predicting per-base accessibility profiles, scoring in silico variant effects at GWAS or rare-variant SNPs, discovering motifs via DeepLIFT/TF-MoDISco from a trained model, or generating cell-type-specific accessibility predictions for unobserved cell states. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/atac-seq/deep-learning-atac) · ★1)
- **atac-seq-differential-accessibility**: Identify differentially accessible chromatin regions across conditions using DiffBind, csaw, DESeq2, or edgeR. Use when comparing ATAC-seq accessibility between treatment groups, choosing between consensus-peak vs sliding-window approaches, picking the correct normalization (full library vs reads-in-peaks), correcting batch with SVA/RUVseq, or interpreting log2FC and FDR thresholds in a chromatin context. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/atac-seq/differential-accessibility) · ★1)
- **atac-seq-differential-accessibility-peak-classification**: Use when comparing two ATAC-seq aging signatures (e.g., male vs female donors) on a shared peak set, needing a between-sex correlation of log2 fold-changes plus per-sex opening/closing DA peak counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/atac-seq-differential-accessibility-peak-classification))
- **atac-seq-differential-accessibility-scoring**: Use when you have ATAC-seq (or similar accessibility) data for two cell types and want sequences ±100 bp around an anchor motif scored by a log2 fold-change so downstream motif analysis can test whether motif spacing correlates with cell-type-specific accessibility. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/atac-seq-differential-accessibility-scoring))
- **atac-seq-ecdna-detection**: Use when bulk ATAC-Seq datasets (tumor samples, cell lines, or normal negative controls) need screening for ecDNA/amplified circular structures and oncogene content, especially when WGS data or copy-number inputs are unavailable. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/atac-seq-ecdna-detection))
- **atac-seq-enhancer-gene-linking**: Predict enhancer-gene regulatory connections from ATAC-seq using ABC, ENCODE-rE2G, HiChIP, or Cicero. Use when linking distal enhancers to target genes, choosing between contact-aware (ABC, ENCODE-rE2G), accessibility-only (Cicero), and orthogonal (HiChIP H3K27ac, EpiMap) approaches, validating predictions against CRISPRi-FlowFISH gold-standard, or building cell-type-specific regulatory maps for fine-mapping or therapeutic target discovery. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/atac-seq/enhancer-gene-linking) · ★1)
- **atac-seq-feature-matrix-construction**: Use when when you have processed scATAC-seq data (peak calling complete, cell-barcode matrix generated) and need to register it into ArchR for downstream multiome analysis alongside scRNA-seq gene expression data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/atac-seq-feature-matrix-construction))
- **atac-seq-footprint-scoring**: Use when you have completed Tn5 insertion bias correction on ATAC-seq reads and now need to quantify footprint signal strength (signal depletion around TF-bound sites) across accessible chromatin regions before classifying individual TF binding sites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/atac-seq-footprint-scoring))
- **atac-seq-footprinting**: Detect transcription factor binding footprints in ATAC-seq using TOBIAS, HINT-ATAC, Wellington, or scprinter. Use when identifying bound TF sites within accessible regions, correcting Tn5 insertion bias before footprinting, choosing between cleavage-based and aggregate-based footprinters, or comparing differential TF activity between conditions. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/atac-seq/footprinting) · ★1)
- **atac-seq-gene-activity-matrix-preprocessing**: Use when integrating scATAC-seq data with annotated scRNA-seq data via scBridge (or similar transfer-learning pipelines) and the target is only a peak-by-cell matrix that must first become a gene activity matrix suitable for cross-omics label transfer. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/atac-seq-gene-activity-matrix-preprocessing))
- **atac-seq-motif-deviation**: Analyze TF motif accessibility variability across samples or single cells using chromVAR. Use when identifying TF motifs whose accessibility correlates with conditions, computing per-sample motif z-scores after matched background correction, comparing to ArchR / Signac equivalents, or distinguishing motif-accessibility signal from per-site footprinting. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/atac-seq/motif-deviation) · ★1)
- **atac-seq-nucleosome-positioning**: Map nucleosome center positions, occupancy, and fuzziness from ATAC-seq fragment-size patterns using NucleoATAC, ATACseqQC, DANPOS3, or scprinter. Use when characterizing nucleosome organization at promoters and enhancers, calling +1/-1 nucleosomes flanking NFRs, generating V-plots for chromatin structure visualization, or comparing nucleosome positioning between conditions. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/atac-seq/nucleosome-positioning) · ★1)
- **atac-seq-peak-annotation**: Use when after differential peak analysis (tl.diff_test) has identified peaks that differ in accessibility across cell types or conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/atac-seq-peak-annotation))
- **atac-seq-peak-binarization**: Use when a raw scATAC-seq peak-by-cell count matrix (AnnData, cells × peaks) must be prepared for a variational autoencoder input — binarization, 0.5% peak-frequency filtering, TF-IDF, and [0,1] scaling are needed. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/atac-seq-peak-binarization))
- **atac-seq-peak-liftover**: Use when query genomic intervals (e.g., human brain ATAC-seq peaks in hg38) must be mapped to another species' genome (e.g., zebrafish rn6 via a UCSC reciprocal- chain file) and scored for regulatory conservation rather than mapped by sequence alone. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/atac-seq-peak-liftover))
- **atac-seq-peak-query-processing**: Use when a set of genomic intervals (e.g. mouse mm10 ATAC-seq peaks) must be mapped to orthologous candidate target regions in another species (e.g. human hg38) with epigenomic and sequence-grammar prioritization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/atac-seq-peak-query-processing))
- **atac-seq-peak-set-analysis**: Use when you have a set of ATAC-seq peaks or a DiffBind differential accessibility report (e.g. Fold/FDR columns from a TR knockout in mouse thymocytes) and need to identify or rank transcriptional regulators from the region coordinates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/atac-seq-peak-set-analysis))
- **atac-seq-peak-to-tss-annotation**: Use when building a candidate TF–target GRN mask from ATAC-seq peaks: motif sites in peaks must be mapped to nearby genes and intersected with an expressed-gene set to seed regulatory network inference (e.g., scKINETICS priors). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/atac-seq-peak-to-tss-annotation))
- **atac-seq-replicate-sample-sheet-configuration**: Use when setting up a bulk ATAC-seq differential accessibility comparison from perturbation (e.g., cKO-vs-WT) experiments with ≥2 replicates per condition and processed BAM plus MACS2 narrowPeak files available. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/atac-seq-replicate-sample-sheet-configuration))
- **atac-seq-signal-normalization**: Use when you have aligned ATAC-seq BAM files and need to detect transcription factor binding sites via footprint analysis. The skill is essential because raw Tn5 insertion signal contains systematic bias toward certain DNA sequences; ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/atac-seq-signal-normalization))
- **atac-seq-single-cell-atac**: Process and analyze single-cell ATAC-seq data with Signac, ArchR, SnapATAC2, or Cell Ranger ATAC. Use when handling 10X scATAC or 10X Multiome (paired RNA+ATAC) data, performing per-cell QC, choosing between ArchR/Signac/SnapATAC2 ecosystems, building per-cluster consensus peaksets, integrating with paired scRNA-seq, doublet detection (AMULET vs ArchR vs scDblFinder), or running pseudobulk differential accessibility per cluster. ([BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills](https://github.com/BaranziniLab/biorouter-skills/tree/HEAD/bio-skills/atac-seq/single-cell-atac) · ★1)
- **atac-seq-starter**: Use this starter when a task lands in the `ATAC-seq` frontier leaf and the repository has curated resources but no dedicated runtime implementation yet. - **athena-diagnostics**: Use this skill to investigate and troubleshoot Amazon Athena problems by analyzing query failures, timeout issues, syntax errors, performance degradation, data format problems, partitioning, SerDe errors, workgroup configuration, federated queries, cost optimization, Glue Catalog integration, Lake Formation permissions, IAM access, encryption, and Athena for Apache Spark issues. - **athena-discovery**: 需求分析的主入口（Phase 01）。以統一流程處理 greenfield / 新功能 / 改變需求—— 三者是同一件事，差別只在起始狀態（空 vs 已有 artifacts）。 統一 8 步：Composition → Flow Alignment → Structural Read → Impact Analysis → Behavior Design → Clarify → Quality Gate → Examples 填入（DELEGATE bdd-analysis）。 產出 Execution Plan（Phase 02-08 的 scope 依據）+ Activity Diagrams + Feature Files（含 Examples，可執行）。 保持 Activity、.feature 兩個視圖的一致性（api.yml / erm.dbml 由後續 Phase 處理）。 ([Athena-Git-Group/athena-plugin-dev](https://github.com/Athena-Git-Group/athena-plugin-dev/tree/HEAD/skills/athena-discovery))
- **athena-draft-with-playbook**: Draft outreach to Athena contacts using your company's Messaging playbook and Athena's email writing guide. ([athena-bda/athena-plugin](https://github.com/athena-bda/athena-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/athena/skills/athena-draft-with-playbook) · 4 copies)
- **athena-engine-parity**: Manual-only investigation of one named live, scan, backtest, chart, or payload parity issue. ([damiantrade84-jpg/athena-python](https://github.com/damiantrade84-jpg/athena-python/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/athena-engine-parity))
- **athena-feedback**: 事後回饋 channel（手動入口）。讓使用者把「flow 結束後才發現的問題」記下來、 接回原本那次 run，append 到 .athena/traces/feedback.jsonl，供 athena-hill-climb 自我改進迴圈讀取。回饋是新增一筆指回原 run，絕不改寫 runs.jsonl。獨立於 flow 與 hill-climb，由使用者事後任何時間主動觸發。當使用者說「回饋」「feedback」 「事後回饋」「對 X 那次的結果回饋」「這次 ship 後發現 bug」「覆蓋率太低」 「我把產出重寫了」時觸發。 ([Athena-Git-Group/athena-plugin-dev](https://github.com/Athena-Git-Group/athena-plugin-dev/tree/HEAD/skills/athena-feedback))
- **athena-flow**: Athena 單一入口流程編排器。讓使用者只輸入一次指令，就能依 point -&gt; spec -&gt; plan -&gt; build -&gt; verify -&gt; review -&gt; ship 的 gate 串接流程自動往下走。每一個 stage 都必須用 全新的 agent 執行，以避免 context 過大與污染。當使用者說「一鍵跑流程」「flow」、 「自動接續執行」「每階段新 agent」時觸發。 ([Athena-Git-Group/athena-plugin-dev](https://github.com/Athena-Git-Group/athena-plugin-dev/tree/HEAD/skills/athena-flow))
- **athena-form-activity**: Activity 視圖的 Spec Skill。從 idea 生成 Activity 骨架（.mmd 或 .activity 格式）並連動生成所有綁定檔案（.feature/.md）骨架，不確定處標記便條紙。可被 /discovery 調用，也可獨立使用。支援兩種格式：.mmd（Mermaid flowchart，預設）與 .activity（自訂 DSL）。 ([Athena-Git-Group/athena-plugin-dev](https://github.com/Athena-Git-Group/athena-plugin-dev/tree/HEAD/skills/athena-form-activity))
- **athena-form-bdd-analysis**: 系統抽象驅動的 BDD 分析（Reconciler）。從已確認的 .feature Rule 骨架推導系統抽象、 實體句型模型與覆蓋矩陣，再據此產出最小必要句型集的 .feature 檔案（含 Examples）。 以 desired-state reconciliation 模式運作：三階段各自讀取現有 artifact → 推導 desired state → 計算 diff → 增量更新。Greenfield 時 current = 空。 善用 Scenario Outline + Examples 做 Data-Driven 測試， 善用 Data Table 承載結構化輸入與驗證。 支援 Boundary 偵測——偵測到 Web Backend 時載入內建 preset（句型分析方針 + Handler 決策樹）。 由 /athena-discovery 在 Strategic 完成後、Tactical 開始前 DELEGATE 觸發。 ([Athena-Git-Group/athena-plugin-dev](https://github.com/Athena-Git-Group/athena-plugin-dev/tree/HEAD/skills/athena-form-bdd-analysis))
- **athena-form-feature-spec**: Feature 視圖的 Spec Skill。從 .feature 骨架（含便條紙）、ES spec 或 User idea 出發，澄清並產出完整的 Gherkin Feature File。可被 /discovery 調用，也可獨立使用。 ([Athena-Git-Group/athena-plugin-dev](https://github.com/Athena-Git-Group/athena-plugin-dev/tree/HEAD/skills/athena-form-feature-spec))
- **athena-framework**: Use this skill when working with Athena Framework for Crystal. Athena is a modular ecosystem of independent, reusable components including: Framework (ATH) for web apps, DependencyInjection (ADI) for IoC containers, Routing (ART) for HTTP routing, Serializer (ASR) for object serialization, Validator (AVD) for validation, Console (ACON) for CLI tools, EventDispatcher (AED) for event-driven architecture, and more. Use for building Crystal web applications, REST APIs, CLI tools, or integrating individual components. ([diegosouzapw/awesome-omni-skill](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/awesome-omni-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/development/athena-framework) · ★67)
- **athena-hill-climb**: Athena 自我改進迴圈（Loop 3 / Hill Climbing）。讀累積的 Run Trace，找出**系統性**失敗模式， 產出對 skill / point rubric / prompt / stage contract / memory 的改進提案（✅/🟡/💡）， 並用既有 athena-skill-eval / athena-skill-audit 驗證「改完有沒有比較好」。一律人工 gate—— 只提案、絕不自動改 system；採納的提案走 /athena-flow（dogfooding）。追蹤 flow-health metrics 看趨勢。獨立於 flow，由 nightly-retro trigger 或「累積 N 筆新 trace」門檻觸發。當使用者說 「跑 retro」「分析 trace」「hill climb」「flow 哪裡常出錯」「自我改進」時觸發。 ([Athena-Git-Group/athena-plugin-dev](https://github.com/Athena-Git-Group/athena-plugin-dev/tree/HEAD/skills/athena-hill-climb))
